from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional

# Record structures (using dataclasses)
@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    """CUSTOMER-MASTER record structure"""
    cust_id: str
    cust_credit_score: int
    cust_total_loans: Decimal
    cust_total_balance: Decimal
    cust_risk_rating: str


@dataclass
class AccountMaster:
    """ACCOUNT-MASTER record structure"""
    acct_id: str
    acct_overdraft_limit: Decimal
    acct_balance: Decimal


@dataclass
class LoanMaster:
    """LOAN-MASTER record structure"""
    loan_payment_amount: Decimal
    loan_collateral_value: Decimal
    loan_current_balance: Decimal
    loan_ltv_ratio: Decimal


@dataclass
class InsuranceMaster:
    """INSURANCE-MASTER record structure"""
    ins_life: bool
    ins_health: bool
    ins_auto: bool
    ins_home: bool
    ins_umbrella: bool
    ins_coverage_amount: Decimal
    ins_claims_count: int
    ins_premium_amount: Decimal


@dataclass
class InvestmentMaster:
    """INVESTMENT-MASTER record structure"""
    inv_quantity: int
    inv_current_price: Decimal
    inv_purchase_price: Decimal
    inv_market_value: Decimal
    inv_gain_loss: Decimal
    inv_dividend_rate: Decimal
    inv_stocks: bool
    inv_bonds: bool
    inv_mutual_fund: bool


@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    """TRANSACTION-LOG record structure"""
    tran_timestamp: str
    tran_type: str
    tran_amount: Decimal
    tran_status: str


@dataclass
class AuditTrail:
    """AUDIT-TRAIL record structure"""
    aud_timestamp: str

def calculate_premiums() -> None:
    """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS.
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM
                PERFORM 4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR
                PERFORM 4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False
    while not WS_EOF:
        insurance_master = read_insurance_master_next()
        if insurance_master is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium()
            apply_risk_factor()
            calculate_final_premium()

def determine_base_premium() -> None:
    """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM.
    EVALUATE TRUE
        WHEN INS-LIFE
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-LIFE-RATE-PER-1000
        WHEN INS-HEALTH
            MOVE WS-HEALTH-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
        WHEN INS-AUTO
            MOVE WS-AUTO-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
        WHEN INS-HOME
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-HOME-RATE-PER-1000
        WHEN INS-UMBRELLA
            MOVE WS-UMBRELLA-RATE TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
    END-EVALUATE."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, INSURANCE_MASTER
    if INSURANCE_MASTER is None:
        return

    match True:
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_life:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_coverage_amount / Decimal("1000") * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_health:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_auto:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_home:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_coverage_amount / Decimal("1000") * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_umbrella:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
        case _:
            pass

def apply_risk_factor() -> None:
    """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR.
    IF INS-CLAIMS-COUNT > 2
        COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = WS-CALC-AMOUNT * 1.25
    END-IF."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, INSURANCE_MASTER
    if INSURANCE_MASTER is None:
        return
    if INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium() -> None:
    """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM.
    MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO INS-PREMIUM-AMOUNT
    ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-PREMIUMS."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS, INSURANCE_MASTER
    if INSURANCE_MASTER is None:
        return
    INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_premium_amount = WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_portfolio_value() -> None:
    """5200-CALCULATE-PORTFOLIO-VALUE.
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE
                PERFORM 5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS
                PERFORM 5230-UPDATE-TOTALS
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""

            calculate_position_value()

def calculate_position_value() -> None:
    """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE.
    COMPUTE INV-MARKET-VALUE = 
        INV-QUANTITY * INV-CURRENT-PRICE."""
    global INV_MARKET_VALUE, INVESTMENT_MASTER
    if INVESTMENT_MASTER is None:
        return
    INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_quantity * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss() -> None:
    """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS.
    COMPUTE INV-GAIN-LOSS = 
        INV-MARKET-VALUE - (INV-QUANTITY * INV-PURCHASE-PRICE)."""
    global INV_GAIN_LOSS, INVESTMENT_MASTER
    if INVESTMENT_MASTER is None:
        return
    INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_gain_loss = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value - (INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_quantity * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_purchase_price)

def update_totals() -> None:
    """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS.
    ADD INV-MARKET-VALUE TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS, INVESTMENT_MASTER
    if INVESTMENT_MASTER is None:
        return
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value

def calculate_dividends() -> None:
    """5400-CALCULATE-DIVIDENDS.
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                IF INV-DIVIDEND-RATE > 0
                    PERFORM 5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND
                    PERFORM 5420-POST-DIVIDEND
                END-IF
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_master = read_investment_master_next()
        if investment_master is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            if INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_dividend_rate > 0:
                compute_dividend()
                post_dividend()

def compute_dividend() -> None:
    """5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND.
    COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
        INV-MARKET-VALUE * INV-DIVIDEND-RATE / 4."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, INVESTMENT_MASTER
    if INVESTMENT_MASTER is None:
        return
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_dividend_rate / Decimal("4.0")

def post_dividend() -> None:
    """5420-POST-DIVIDEND.
    ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DIVIDENDS."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS
    WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def daily_summary() -> None:
    """6100-DAILY-SUMMARY.
    DISPLAY "GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY..."
    MOVE SPACES TO REPORT-LINE
    STRING "MEGA-ENTERPRISE DAILY SUMMARY - " DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-CURRENT-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO REPORT-LINE
    WRITE REPORT-LINE
    PERFORM 6110-WRITE-TOTALS."""

    global REPORT_LINE
    REPORT_LINE = ""
    REPORT_LINE = f"MEGA-ENTERPRISE DAILY SUMMARY - {WS_CURRENT_DATE}"

def write_totals() -> None:
    """6110-WRITE-TOTALS.
    MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
    STRING "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO REPORT-LINE
    WRITE REPORT-LINE
    
    MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
    STRING "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO REPORT-LINE
    WRITE REPORT-LINE
    
    MOVE WS-TOTAL-LOANS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
    STRING "TOTAL LOANS: " DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO REPORT-LINE
    WRITE REPORT-LINE."""
    global REPORT_LINE, WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)
    REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL DEPOSITS: {WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"
    write_report_line(REPORT_LINE)

    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
    REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: {WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"
    write_report_line(REPORT_LINE)

    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(WS_TOTAL_LOANS)
    REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL LOANS: {WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"
    write_report_line(REPORT_LINE)

def write_transaction() -> None:
    """8100-WRITE-TRANSACTION.
    MOVE WS-CURRENT-TIMESTAMP TO TRAN-TIMESTAMP
    MOVE 'DEP' TO TRAN-TYPE
    MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO TRAN-AMOUNT
    MOVE 'C' TO TRAN-STATUS
    WRITE TRANSACTION-RECORD."""
    global TRAN_TIMESTAMP, TRAN_TYPE, TRAN_AMOUNT, TRAN_STATUS, WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, WS_CALC_AMOUNT, TRANSACTION_RECORD
    TRAN_TIMESTAMP = WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    TRAN_TYPE = 'DEP'
    TRAN_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    TRAN_STATUS = 'C'
    TRANSACTION_RECORD = TransactionLog(tran_timestamp=TRAN_TIMESTAMP, tran_type=TRAN_TYPE, tran_amount=TRAN_AMOUNT, tran_status=TRAN_STATUS)

    if TRANSACTION_RECORD:
        write_transaction_record(TRANSACTION_RECORD)

def write_audit() -> None:
    """8200-WRITE-AUDIT.
    MOVE WS-CURRENT-TIMESTAMP TO AUD-TIMESTAMP
    WRITE AUDIT-RECORD."""
    global AUD_TIMESTAMP, WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, AUDIT_RECORD
    AUD_TIMESTAMP = WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    AUDIT_RECORD = AuditTrail(aud_timestamp=AUD_TIMESTAMP)
    if AUDIT_RECORD:
        write_audit_record(AUDIT_RECORD)

def format_date() -> None:
    """8300-FORMAT-DATE.
    STRING WS-TEMP-DATE(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
           '-' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-TEMP-DATE(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
           '-' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-TEMP-DATE(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE
           INTO WS-FORMATTED-DATE."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_DATE, WS_TEMP_DATE
    WS_FORMATTED_DATE = f"{WS_TEMP_DATE[0:4]}-{WS_TEMP_DATE[4:6]}-{WS_TEMP_DATE[6:8]}"

def validate_account() -> None:
    """8400-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT.
    SET WS-VALID TO TRUE
    IF ACCT-ID = SPACES
        SET WS-INVALID TO TRUE
    END-IF."""
    global WS_VALID, WS_INVALID, ACCT_ID
    WS_VALID = True
    if ACCT_ID == "":  # Assuming SPACES is an empty string
        WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax() -> None:
    """8500-CALCULATE-TAX.
    EVALUATE TRUE
        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-1-MAX
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE
        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                 WS-BRACKET-2-RATE)
        WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                ((WS-BRACKET-2-MAX - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                 WS-BRACKET-2-RATE) +
                ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-2-MAX) * 
                 WS-BRACKET-3-RATE)
        WHEN OTHER
            COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-5-RATE
    END-EVALUATE."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_CALC_TAX
    match True:
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                           ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                           ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                           ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def termination() -> None:
    """9000-TERMINATION.
    PERFORM 9100-CLOSE-FILES
    PERFORM 9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS
    DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY"."""
    close_files()
    display_statistics()
    print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

def close_files() -> None:
    """9100-CLOSE-FILES.
    CLOSE CUSTOMER-MASTER
    CLOSE ACCOUNT-MASTER
    CLOSE LOAN-MASTER
    CLOSE INSURANCE-MASTER
    CLOSE INVESTMENT-MASTER
    CLOSE TRANSACTION-LOG

    close_file("CUSTOMER-MASTER")
    close_file("ACCOUNT-MASTER")
    close_file("LOAN-MASTER")
    close_file("INSURANCE-MASTER")
    close_file("INVESTMENT-MASTER")
    close_file("TRANSACTION-LOG")
    close_file("AUDIT-TRAIL")
    close_file("REPORT-FILE")

# === CHUNK 3 (lines 1337-2004) ===
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal(0)
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_id: str = ""
    cust_name: str = ""
    cust_last_name: str = ""
    cust_state: str = ""
    cust_last_activity: int = 0

        self.ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal(0)

        self.ws_eof: bool = False

        self.ws_process_count: int = 0

        self.loan_delinquent: bool = False

        self.acct_min_balance: Decimal = Decimal(0)

        self.customer_master = CustomerMaster()

    def customer_segmentation(self):
        """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
        print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...") # 9311
        self.ws_not_eof = True # 9312
        while not self.ws_eof: # 9313
            # READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
            # Simulating reading from a customer master file
            if self.customer_master is None: # 9315
                self.ws_eof = True # 9316
            else: # 9317
                self.calculate_clv() # 9318
                self.assign_segment() # 9319
        # END-PERFORM is implicit

    def calculate_clv(self):
        """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
        self.ws_calc_result = (self.customer_master.cust_total_balance * self.ws_savings_rate) + \
                             (self.customer_master.cust_total_loans * self.ws_personal_rate) + \
                             (self.customer_master.cust_total_investments * Decimal(0.01)) # 9323

    def assign_segment(self):
        """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
        if self.ws_calc_result > 10000: # 9326
            self.ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM' # 9327
        elif self.ws_calc_result > 5000: # 9328
            self.ws_temp_code = 'GOLD' # 9329
        elif self.ws_calc_result > 1000: # 9330
            self.ws_temp_code = 'SILVER' # 9331
        else: # 9332
            self.ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE' # 9333

    def sweep_accounts(self):
        """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS."""
        if self.acct_balance > self.acct_min_balance: # 9632
            self.ws_calc_amount = self.acct_balance - self.acct_min_balance # 9633
            self.acct_balance -= self.ws_calc_amount # 9634
            self.ws_total_investments += self.ws_calc_amount # 9635

import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional, List

@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    """Represents a transaction log record."""
    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class Customer:
    """Represents a customer record."""
    cust_ssn: str = ""
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_risk_rating: str = ""

# Global variables (Work Storage)
CUST_STATUS: str = ""
WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
WS_EOF: bool = False
WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
TRAN_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
LOAN_PAID_OFF: bool = False
WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
WS_TEMP_STRING: str = ""
WS_VALID: bool = False
WS_ATM_FEE_FOREIGN: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_FORMATTED_COUNT: str = ""
WS_CUST_COUNT: int = 0

# Instance of data records
transaction_log = TransactionLog()
customer = Customer()

def c100_transaction_monitoring():
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")  # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    WS_NOT_EOF = True  # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    WS_EOF = False
    while not WS_EOF:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        try:
            transaction_log = TransactionLog()  # READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
            WS_EOF = False
            c110_rule_based_detection()  # PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
            c120_behavior_analysis()  # PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
            c130_network_analysis()  # PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS
        except StopIteration:  # AT END
            WS_EOF = True  # SET WS-EOF TO TRUE

def c110_rule_based_detection():
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if transaction_log.tran_amount >= 10000:  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        c111_flag_ctr()  # PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    if 5000 <= transaction_log.tran_amount < 10000:  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        c112_check_structuring()  # PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING

def c111_flag_ctr():
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT.

def c112_check_structuring():
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT
    WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT.

def d110_classification():
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    global CUST_STATUS
    if customer.cust_credit_score > 750:  # IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif customer.cust_credit_score > 650:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'B'  # MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif customer.cust_credit_score > 550:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'C'  # MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    else:  # ELSE
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING

def d120_regression():
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (customer.cust_credit_score * 10) + \
                     (customer.cust_total_balance / 1000) - \
                     (customer.cust_total_loans / 2000)  # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) + (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) - (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000).

        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""

        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'

    def initialization(self):

        self.WS_WORK_AREAS = {}  # INITIALIZE WS-WORK-AREAS

        self.WS_CURR_MONTH = self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME[4:6]  # MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO RPT-MONTH
        self.WS_CURR_DAY = self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME[6:8]  # MOVE WS-CURR-DAY   TO RPT-DAY
        self.RPT_YEAR = self.WS_CURR_YEAR

    def initialize_tables(self):
        """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES."""
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            # INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = RateTableEntry()
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = ""  # MOVE SPACES TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)

            # INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
            self.BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = BranchTableEntry()

    def load_reference_data(self):
        """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
        self.WS_TBL_IDX = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and self.WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            # READ REFERENCE-FILE INTO WS-REF-RECORD
            #Assuming a placeholder for reading from file.
            if self.WS_TBL_IDX > 20:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else:
                self.WS_REF_RECORD = ReferenceFileRecord(WS_REF_CODE = f"CODE{self.WS_TBL_IDX}", WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(str(self.WS_TBL_IDX) + ".00"))
                self.WS_REF_CODE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE  # MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                self.WS_REF_RATE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE  # MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = self.WS_REF_CODE
                self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = self.WS_REF_RATE
                self.WS_TBL_IDX += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def process_transactions(self):
        """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS."""
        # READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANSACTION-REC
        # AT END
        #     MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        # NOT AT END
        #Placeholder for file read
        if self.WS_TRANS_COUNT > 5:
           self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
           return
        self.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
        self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC = TransactionFileRecord(TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = "12345", TXN_AMOUNT = Decimal("100.00"), TXN_TYPE = "D", TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = "67890")
        self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
        self.TXN_AMOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT
        self.TXN_TYPE = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE
        self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT
        self.validate_transaction()  # PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
        if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.process_by_type()  # PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
        else:
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def validate_transaction(self):
        """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        if self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "" or self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "00000":  # IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'  # MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        try:
            float(self.TXN_AMOUNT)
        except ValueError:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
        #The following line was changed from "!=" to "not in"
        if self.TXN_TYPE not in ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']:  # IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'  # MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        self.validate_account_exists()  # PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
        self.validate_business_rules()  # PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES

    def validate_account_exists(self):
        """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def validate_business_rules(self):
        """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
        if self.TXN_TYPE == 'W':  # IF TXN-TYPE = 'W'
            if self.TXN_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
                self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
                self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        if self.TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal("1000000.00"):  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > 1000000
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'  # MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def process_by_type(self):
        """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
        match self.TXN_TYPE:  # EVALUATE TXN-TYPE
            case 'D':  # WHEN 'D'
                self.process_deposit()  # PERFORM 2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT
            case 'W':  # WHEN 'W'
                self.process_withdrawal()  # PERFORM 2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL
            case 'T':  # WHEN 'T'
                self.process_transfer()  # PERFORM 2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER
            case 'I':  # WHEN 'I'
                self.process_interest()  # PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-INTEREST
            case _:  # WHEN OTHER
                self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def process_deposit(self):
        """2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'  # MOVE 'DEPOSIT' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS
        self.WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-DEPOSIT-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def update_account(self):
        """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
        self.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = "20240101"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ACCT-LAST-UPDATE
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = "20240101"
        # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD - Assuming file operation is handled externally
        self.WS_FILE_STATUS = "00" # placeholder
        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'  # MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def write_audit_trail(self):
        """2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
        self.AUDIT_RECORD = AuditRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_ACCOUNT = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO AUDIT-ACCOUNT
        self.AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_AMOUNT = self.TXN_AMOUNT # MOVE TXN-AMOUNT TO AUDIT-AMOUNT
        self.AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TYPE = self.TXN_TYPE # MOVE TXN-TYPE TO AUDIT-TYPE
        self.AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = "20240101" # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
        self.AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_JOB_ID = self.WS_JOB_ID # MOVE WS-JOB-ID TO AUDIT-JOB-ID
        # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD - Assuming file operation is handled externally
        # Placeholder for file write.
        if self.AUDIT_COUNT < 100:
            self.AUDIT_ENTRY[self.AUDIT_COUNT] = str(self.AUDIT_RECORD)
            self.AUDIT_COUNT += 1

    def process_withdrawal(self):
        """2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'  # MOVE 'WITHDRAWAL' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS
        self.WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-WITHDRAWAL-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL
        if self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < self.WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:  # IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE < WS-MIN-BALANCE-LIMIT
            self.generate_low_balance_alert()  # PERFORM 2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT

    def generate_low_balance_alert(self):
        """2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT."""
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD = AlertRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-ALERT-RECORD
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_TYPE = 'LOW-BAL' # MOVE 'LOW-BAL' TO ALERT-TYPE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_ACCOUNT = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ALERT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ALERT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_DATE = "20240101"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ALERT-DATE
        # WRITE ALERT-RECORD FROM WS-ALERT-RECORD - Assuming file operation is handled externally
        #Placeholder for file write
        self.WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ALERT-COUNT

    def process_transfer(self):
        """2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER."""
        self.validate_target_account()  # PERFORM 2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.debit_source()  # PERFORM 2520-DEBIT-SOURCE
            self.credit_target()  # PERFORM 2530-CREDIT-TARGET
            self.record_transfer()  # PERFORM 2540-RECORD-TRANSFER
        else:
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def validate_target_account(self):
        """2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def debit_source(self):
        """2520-DEBIT-SOURCE."""
        self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-SOURCE-BALANCE
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-SOURCE-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD - Assuming file operation is handled externally

    def credit_target(self):
        """2530-CREDIT-TARGET."""
        self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TARGET-BALANCE
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_ID = self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO ACCT-ID
        # READ MASTER-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC - Assuming file operation is handled externally
        self.ACCOUNT_RECORD.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-TARGET-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD - Assuming file operation is handled externally

    def record_transfer(self):
        """2540-RECORD-TRANSFER."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANSFERS
        self.WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANSFER-COUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def process_interest(self):
        """2600-PROCESS-INTEREST."""
        # COMPUTE WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * WS-INTEREST-RATE / 100
        self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * self.WS_INTEREST_RATE / 100
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-

class WSBeneficiary:
    """10 WS-BENEFICIARY OCCURS 5 TIMES."""

        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_VEHICLE_AGE: int = 0

    def calculate_pmi(self) -> None:
        """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
                )  # 10337
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
                )  # 10340
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
                )  # 10343
            case _:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0050") / 12
                )  # 10346

    def evaluate_history(self) -> None:
        """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
        if self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50  # 10349
            self.WS_FACTOR_1 = "SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY"  # 10350
        if self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30  # 10353
            self.WS_FACTOR_2 = "60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES"  # 10354
        if self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20  # 10357
            self.WS_FACTOR_3 = "MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES"  # 10358

    def calculate_final_risk(self) -> None:
        """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE = self.WS_RISK_SCORE / 4  # 10361
        match True:
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "LOW RISK"  # 10364
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "MODERATE"  # 10366
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "ELEVATED"  # 10368
            case _:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = "HIGH RISK"  # 10370

    def determine_approval(self) -> None:
        """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
        if self.WS_CREDIT_TIER == "F":
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "D"  # 10403
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = "CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW"  # 10404
            return  # 10405
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == "HIGH RISK":
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "D"  # 10408
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = "RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED"  # 10409
            return  # 10410
        if self.WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "D"  # 10413
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = "DTI RATIO TOO HIGH"  # 10414
            return  # 10415
        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = "A"  # 10417
        self.calculate_approved_terms()  # 10418

    def calculate_approved_terms(self) -> None:
        """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT  # 10451
        match self.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
            case "A":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = (
                    self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.00")
                )  # 10454
            case "B":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = (
                    self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.50")
                )  # 10456
            case "C":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = (
                    self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("1.50")
                )  # 10458
            case "D":
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = (
                    self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("3.00")
                )  # 10460
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == "ELEVATED":
            self.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal("0.50")  # 10464

    def generate_loan_terms(self) -> None:
        """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = self.WS_APPROVED_RATE  # 10500
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200  # 10502
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (
            1 + self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
        ) ** self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS  # 10504
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (
            self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
            * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
            * self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR
            / (self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)
        )  # 10506
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT  # 10509

    def create_amortization(self) -> None:
        """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT  # 10601
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = date.today()  # 10602
        self.WS_AMORT_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_AMORT_IDX <= self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS:
            self.calculate_payment_split()  # 10605
            self.WS_AMORT_IDX += 1  # 10604

    def calculate_payment_split(self) -> None:
        """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
        self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
            self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
        )  # 10652
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
            self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1]
        )  # 10654
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[
            self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1
        ]  # 10656
        self.AMORT_BALANCE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE  # 10658
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = self.WS_AMORT_IDX  # 10659
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
            self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        )  # 10661
        if self.LOAN_MORTGAGE:
            self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
                self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM
            ) / 12  # 10663
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
                self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
                + self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1]
                + self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT
            )  # 10665
        else:
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (
                self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
            )  # 10669
        self.advance_payment_date()  # 10670

    def advance_payment_date(self) -> None:
        """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1  # 10673
        if self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
            self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1  # 10675
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1  # 10676
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = date(
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR, self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH, 1
        )  # 10678

    def finalize_loan(self) -> None:
        """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = date.today()  # 10701
        self.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = date.today()  # Dummy
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = "A"  # 10704
        self.create_loan_record()  # 10705
        self.disburse_funds()  # 10706
        self.send_confirmation()  # 10707

    def create_loan_record(self) -> None:
        """10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD."""
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD = WsLoanRecord()  # 10751
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID  # 10752
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_type = self.WS_LOAN_TYPE  # 10753
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_amount = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT  # 10754
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_rate = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE  # 10755
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_payment = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT  # 10756
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_start = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE  # 10757
        self.WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_status = self.WS_LOAN_STATUS  # 10758
        # WRITE LOAN-RECORD FROM WS-LOAN-RECORD. # 10759 (Simulated Write)
        print(f"Loan Record: {self.WS_LOAN_RECORD}")

    def disburse_funds(self) -> None:
        """10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS."""
        self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT  # 10761
        self.process_deposit()  # 10762
        self.write_audit_trail()  # 10763

    def send_confirmation(self) -> None:
        """10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = "LOAN-CONFIRM"  # 10771
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = "EMAIL"  # 10772
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = "Your loan has been approved"  # 10773
        self.send_notification()  # 10774

    def process_decline(self) -> None:
        """10800-PROCESS-DECLINE."""
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = "DECLINED"  # 10801
        self.record_decline()  # 10802
        self.send_decline_notice()  # 10803

    def record_decline(self) -> None:
        """10810-RECORD-DECLINE."""
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecord()  # 10811

        # WRITE DECLINE-RECORD FROM WS-DECLINE-RECORD. # 10816 (Simulated Write)
        print(f"Decline Record: {self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD}")

    def send_decline_notice(self) -> None:

        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = "LOAN-DECLINE"  # 10821

        self.send_notification()  # 10825

    # Placeholder methods for external calls

    def portfolio_management(self) -> None:
        """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.load_portfolio()  # 11001
        self.update_market_prices()  # 11002
        self.calculate_values()  # 11003
        self.rebalance_check()  # 11004
        self.generate_statements()  # 11005

    def load_portfolio(self) -> None:
        """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1  # 11101
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = ""
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= 100 and self.WS_EOF_FLAG != "Y":  # 11102
            try:
                # Simulate reading from a file (replace with actual file reading)
                self.WS_HOLDING_REC = self.read_holdings_file() # Replace this with actual file read
                self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1] = self.WS_HOLDING_REC  # 11107
                self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1  # 11108
            except StopIteration:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "Y"  # 11104
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error reading file: {e}")
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = "Y"
        self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1  # 11111

    def update_market_prices(self) -> None:
        """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:  # 11201
            self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1].hold_symbol  # 11202
            self.get_quote()  # 11203
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1].hold_current_price = (
                self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE
            )  # 11204
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1  # 11201

    def get_quote(self) -> None:
        """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
        self.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol = self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL  # 11251
        self.getquote(self.QUOTE_REQUEST, self.QUOTE_RESPONSE) # 11252
        if self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status == "OK":  # 11253
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = self.QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price  # 11254
        else:
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal("0.00")  # 11256

    def calculate_values(self) -> None:
        """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal("0.00")  # 11301
        self.WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal("0.00")  # 11302
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal("0.00")  # 11303
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:  # 11304
            self.calculate_holding_value()  # 11305
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1  # 11304

    def calculate_holding_value(self) -> None:
        """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
        hold_idx = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1
        self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_market_value = (
            self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_shares
            * self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_current_price
        )  # 11351
        self.WS_HOLD_COST = (
            self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_shares
            * self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_cost_per_share
        )  # 11354
        self.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx

def calc_auto_premium(data: Data) -> None:
    """Translated COBOL code."""
    match data.WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10: # Line numbers not applicable here as it's a section
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 100 # ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 50 # ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25: # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

    if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0: # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
        data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200) # COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE # ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

    if data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0: # IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
        data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100) # COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE # ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / 12 # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.

def calc_home_premium(data: Data) -> None:
    """13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM."""
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003") # COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
    match data.WS_HOME_AGE: # EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10: # WHEN 0 THRU 10
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25: # WHEN 11 THRU 25
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50: # WHEN 26 THRU 50
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2") # MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    if data.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y': # IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    if data.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y': # IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50 # COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT # SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    if data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200: # IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("200.00") # MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / 12 # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.

def calc_health_premium(data: Data) -> None:
    """13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM."""
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.00") # MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    match data.WS_INSURED_AGE: # EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18: # WHEN 0 THRU 18
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5") # MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30: # WHEN 19 THRU 30
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40: # WHEN 31 THRU 40
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3") # MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50: # WHEN 41 THRU 50
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6") # MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60: # WHEN 51 THRU 60
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0") # MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8") # MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    match data.WS_PLAN_TYPE: # EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
        case 'BRONZE': # WHEN 'BRONZE'
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8") # MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case 'SILVER': # WHEN 'SILVER'
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case 'GOLD': # WHEN 'GOLD'
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3") # MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        case 'PLATINUM': # WHEN 'PLATINUM'
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6") # MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    if data.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y': # IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5") # MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * 12 # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.

def underwriting(data: Data) -> None:
    """13300-UNDERWRITING."""
    evaluate_risk_factors(data) # PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
    check_medical_history(data) # PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
    verify_information(data) # PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
    determine_decision(data) # PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION

def evaluate_risk_factors(data: Data) -> None:
    """13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS."""
    data.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if data.POLICY_LIFE: # IF POLICY-LIFE
        if data.WS_BMI > 30: # IF WS-BMI > 30
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10 # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        
        if data.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y': # IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25 # ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        
        if data.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y': # IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15 # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    
    if data.POLICY_AUTO: # IF POLICY-AUTO
        if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21: # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20 # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        
        if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1: # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15 # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

def check_medical_history(data: Data) -> None:
    """13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY."""
    if data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0: # IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
        data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = Decimal(data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5) # COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += int(data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS) # ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if data.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y': # IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10 # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    if data.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5: # IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5 # ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

def verify_information(data: Data) -> None:
    """13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION."""
    check_fraud_indicators(data) # PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
    validate_documents(data) # PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS

def check_fraud_indicators(data: Data) -> None:
    """13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS."""
    if data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3: # IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20 # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        data.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
    
    if data.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y': # IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10 # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

def validate_documents(data: Data) -> None:
    """13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS."""
    if data.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y': # IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING' # MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
    else: # ELSE
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE' # MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS

def determine_decision(data: Data) -> None:
    """13340-DETERMINE-DECISION."""
    match True: # EVALUATE TRUE
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50: # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE' # MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30: # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD' # MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15: # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD' # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED' # MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9

def issue_policy(data: Data) -> None:
    """13400-ISSUE-POLICY."""
    if data.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE': # IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
        generate_policy_number(data) # PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
        create_policy_record(data) # PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
        set_beneficiaries(data) # PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
        send_policy_docs(data) # PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
    else: # ELSE
        send_decline_letter(data) # PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER

def generate_policy_number(data: Data) -> None:
    """13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER."""
    #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    data.WS_DATE_PART = "CURRENT-DATE" # Replace with actual date function if needed
    data.WS_TYPE_PART = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
    #COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    import random
    data.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999)  # Replace with actual random function if needed
    #STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.
    data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{data.WS_TYPE_PART}{data.WS_DATE_PART}{data.WS_RANDOM_PART}"

    pass

def check_policy_status(data: Data) -> None:
    """13522-CHECK-POLICY-STATUS."""
    if data.WS_POLICY_STATUS != 'A': #IF WS-POLICY-STATUS NOT = 'A'
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED' #MOVE 'DENIED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE' #MOVE 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE' TO WS-CLAIM-DENY-REASON

def check_coverage(data: Data) -> None:
    """13524-CHECK-COVERAGE."""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_TYPE != data.WS_COVERED_PERILS: #IF WS-CLAIM-TYPE NOT = WS-COVERED-PERILS
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED' #MOVE 'DENIED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'NOT COVERED PERIL' #MOVE 'NOT COVERED PERIL' TO WS-CLAIM-DENY-REASON

def check_deductible(data: Data) -> None:
    """13526-CHECK-DEDUCTIBLE."""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT <= data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE: #IF WS-CLAIM-AMOUNT <= WS-DEDUCTIBLE
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED' #MOVE 'DENIED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE' #MOVE 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE' TO WS-CLAIM-DENY-REASON

def move_pep_data(ws_pep_status: str, pep_match_score: Decimal, ws_pep_score: Decimal):
    """Move pep data."""
    ws_pep_status = 'Y'
    ws_pep_score = pep_match_score

#       16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA.
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
#           CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
#           IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0

def check_adverse_media(ws_customer_name: str, media_search_name: str, media_request: any, media_response: any, media_hits_found: int, ws_watchlist_hits: int):
    """Check for adverse media."""
    media_search_name = ws_customer_name
    media_response = mediasrch(media_request) # Assuming mediasrch is a function that handles the call
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        ws_watchlist_hits += media_hits_found
    return media_response, ws_watchlist_hits

def mediasrch(media_request: any):
    """Placeholder for media search function"""
    return None # Replace with actual implementation


#       16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE.
#           IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0
#              ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
#           END-IF
#           IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0
#              ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
#           END-IF
#           COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE = 
#              WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS.
def calculate_match_score(ws_ofac_score: Decimal, ws_pep_score: Decimal, ws_match_score: Decimal, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> Decimal:
    """Calculate the match score."""
    if ws_ofac_score > Decimal('0'):
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > Decimal('0'):
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits if ws_watchlist_hits else Decimal('0')
    return ws_match_score

#       16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION.
#           EVALUATE TRUE
#              WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
#                 MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
#                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
#              WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
#                 MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
#                 MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
#              WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
#                 MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
#                 MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
#              WHEN OTHER
#                 MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
#                 MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
#           END-EVALUATE.
def determine_disposition(ws_match_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """Determine the disposition based on the match score."""
    ws_match_type = ""
    ws_sar_required = ""
    ws_case_status = ""

    if ws_match_score >= Decimal('90'):
        ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
        ws_sar_required = 'Y'
    elif ws_match_score >= Decimal('75'):
        ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
        ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
    elif ws_match_score >= Decimal('50'):
        ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    else:
        ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'

    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status

#       16200-KYC-VERIFICATION.
#           PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
#           PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
#           PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
#           PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
def kyc_verification(id_request: any, id_response: any, addr_request: any, addr_response: any, ws_doc_type: str, passport_req: any, passport_resp: any, license_req: any, license_resp: any, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
    """Perform KYC verification by calling identity, address, and document verification methods."""
    ws_id_status = verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn, ws_customer_dob, ws_customer_name, id_request, id_response)
    ws_addr_status = verify_address(ws_customer_address, addr_request, addr_response)
    ws_doc_status = verify_documents(ws_doc_type, passport_req, passport_resp, license_req, license_resp, ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, ws_license_number, ws_license_state)
    ws_kyc_status = determine_kyc_status(ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status)

    return ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status, ws_kyc_status

#       16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY.
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
#           CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
#           IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'
#              MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
#           ELSE
#              MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
#           END-IF.
def verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, id_request: any, id_response: any) -> str:
    """Verify customer identity."""

    id_response = idverify(id_request, ws_customer_ssn, ws_customer_dob, ws_customer_name) # Assuming idverify is a function that handles the call
    id_verified = id_response.id_verified
    if id_verified == 'Y':
        ws_id_status = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_id_status = 'FAILED'
    return ws_id_status

    """Placeholder for ID verification function"""
    @dataclass
    class IDResponse:
        id_verified: str
    return IDResponse('Y') # Replace with actual implementation

#       16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS.
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
#           CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
#           IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'
#              MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS

#              MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
#           END-IF.
def verify_address(ws_customer_address: str, addr_request: any, addr_response: any) -> str:
    """Verify customer address."""
    addr_response = addrverify(addr_request, ws_customer_address) # Assuming addrverify is a function that handles the call
    addr_verified = addr_response.addr_verified
    if addr_verified == 'Y':
        ws_addr_status = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_addr_status = 'UNVERIFIED'
    return ws_addr_status

#       16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS.
#           IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'
#              PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
#           ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
#              PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
#           ELSE
#              PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
#           END-IF
#           END-IF.
def verify_documents(ws_doc_type: str, passport_req: any, passport_resp: any, license_req: any, license_resp: any, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """Verify customer documents based on document type."""
    if ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':
        ws_doc_status = verify_passport(ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, passport_req, passport_resp)
    elif ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':
        ws_doc_status = verify_license(ws_license_number, ws_license_state, license_req, license_resp)
    else:
        ws_doc_status = verify_other_doc()
    return ws_doc_status

#       16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT.

def verify_passport(ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, passport_req: any, passport_resp: any) -> str:

    if passport_valid == 'Y':

    else:
        ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'
    return ws_doc_status

    else:

#       16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK.

#       16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING.
#           PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY
#           PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS
#           PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK
#           PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
def transaction_monitoring(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str) -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str, int]:
    """Monitor transactions for suspicious activity."""
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold)
    ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device, ws_fraud_score)
    ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review = calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score)

    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_pattern_flag, ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score, ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

#       16410-CHECK-VELOCITY.
#           IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD
#              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
#              ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
#           END-IF
#           IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
#              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
#              ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
#           END-IF.
def check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, int]:
    """Check transaction velocity against thresholds."""
    ws_velocity_flag = 'N'
    ws_amount_flag = 'N'
    ws_fraud_score = 0

    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score

#       16420-CHECK-PATTERNS.
#           IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5
#              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
#              ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
#           END-IF
#           IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'
#              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
#              ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
#           END-IF.
def check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """Check for suspicious transaction patterns."""
    ws_pattern_flag = 'N'

    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 15
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 30

    return ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score

#       16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK.
#           IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'
#              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
#              ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
#           END-IF
#           IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'
#              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
#              ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
#           END-IF.
def check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str, int]:
    """Check for high-risk factors such as country and new device."""
    ws_location_flag = 'N'
    ws_device_flag = 'N'

    if ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
        ws_location_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 25
    if ws_new_device == 'Y':
        ws_device_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 10
    
    return ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score

#       16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
#           EVALUATE TRUE
#              WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
#                 MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
#                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
#              WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
#                 MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
#                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
#              WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
#                 MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
#              WHEN OTHER
#                 MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
#           END-EVALUATE.
def calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Calculate the risk score and determine the fraud decision."""
    ws_fraud_decision = ""
    ws_manual_review = ""

    if ws_fraud_score >= 80:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'
        ws_manual_review = 'Y'
    elif ws_fraud_score >= 60:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
        ws_manual_review = 'Y'
    elif ws_fraud_score >= 40:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
    else:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'

    return ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

#       16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT.
#           IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'
#              PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA
#              PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR
#              PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR
#           END-IF.
def suspicious_activity_report(ws_sar_required: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generate and file a suspicious activity report if required."""
    if ws_sar_required == 'Y':
        sar_subject_name, sar_subject_addr, sar_subject_ssn, sar_amount, sar_activity_date = gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name, ws_customer_address, ws_customer_ssn, ws_transaction_amount)
        sar_rec_name, sar_rec_addr, sar_rec_amount, sar_rec_date, sar_rec_narrative = generate_sar(sar_subject_name, sar_subject_addr, sar_amount, sar_activity_date)
        file_sar(sar_rec_name, sar_rec_addr, sar_rec_amount, sar_rec_date, sar_rec_narrative)

#       16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA.
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO SAR-SUBJECT-NAME
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR
#           MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO SAR-SUBJECT-SSN
#           MOVE WS-TRANSACTION-AMOUNT TO SAR-AMOUNT
#           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE.
def gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, str, Decimal, date]:
    """Gather data for the suspicious activity report."""
    sar_subject_name = ws_customer_name
    sar_subject_addr = ws_customer_address
    sar_subject_ssn = ws_customer_ssn
    sar_amount = ws_transaction_amount
    sar_activity_date = date.today()

    return sar_subject_name, sar_subject_addr, sar_subject_ssn, sar_amount, sar_activity_date

#       16520-GENERATE-SAR.
#           INITIALIZE WS-SAR-RECORD
#           MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-NAME TO SAR-REC-NAME
#           MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR TO SAR-REC-ADDR
#           MOVE SAR-AMOUNT TO SAR-REC-AMOUNT
#           MOVE SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE TO SAR-REC-DATE

def generate_sar(sar_subject_name: str, sar_subject_addr: str, sar_amount: Decimal, sar_activity_date: date) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, date, str]:
    """Generate the suspicious activity report."""

    sar_rec_name = sar_subject_name
    sar_rec_addr = sar_subject_addr
    sar_rec_amount = sar_amount
    sar_rec_date = sar_activity_date
    sar_rec_narrative = 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED'
    
    return sar_rec_name, sar_rec_addr, sar_rec_amount, sar_rec_date, sar_rec_narrative

#       16530-FILE-SAR.
#           MOVE 'PENDING' TO SAR-STATUS
#           WRITE SAR-RECORD FROM WS-SAR-RECORD.
def file_sar(sar_rec_name: str, sar_rec_addr: str, sar_rec_amount: Decimal, sar_rec_date: date, sar_rec_narrative: str) -> None:
    """File the suspicious activity report."""
    sar_status = 'PENDING'

    @dataclass
    class SARRecord:
        sar_rec_name: str
        sar_rec_addr: str
        sar_rec_amount: Decimal
        sar_rec_date: date
        sar_rec_narrative: str
        sar_status: str

    sar_record = SARRecord(sar_rec_name, sar_rec_addr, sar_rec_amount, sar_rec_date, sar_rec_narrative, sar_status)

    write_sar_record(sar_record)

def write_sar_record(sar_record: any) -> None:
    """Placeholder function to write the SAR record."""
    # Replace with actual database write or file write
    print(f"SAR Record Written: {sar_record}")

# CUSTOMER SERVICE PROCEDURES                                    *
#----------------------------------------------------------------*
#       17000-CUSTOMER-SERVICE.
#           PERFORM 17100-CREATE-CASE
#           PERFORM 17200-ROUTE-CASE
#           PERFORM 17300-PROCESS-CASE
#           PERFORM 17400-RESOLVE-CASE
#           PERFORM 17500-FOLLOW-UP.
def customer_service(ws_case_type: str, ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_channel: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_follow_up_required: str) -> None:
    """Process a customer service request."""
    ws_case_id, ws_open_date, ws_case_status, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = create_case(ws_case_type)
    ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent = route_case(ws_case_type)
    ws_interaction_count, ws_research_notes, ws_previous_case_count, ws_caller_type, ws_resolution_code = process_case(ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id, ws_channel, ws_case_type, ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount)
    resolve_case(ws_case_id, ws_resolution_code)
    follow_up(ws_follow_up_required, ws_case_id, ws_customer_account)

#       17100-CREATE-CASE.
#           PERFORM 17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID
#           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-OPEN-DATE
#           MOVE 'OPEN' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
#           PERFORM 17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.
def create_case(ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, date, str, int, int]:
    """Create a new customer service case."""
    ws_case_id = generate_case_id()
    ws_open_date = date.today()
    ws_case_status = 'OPEN'
    ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = categorize_case(ws_case_type, ws_open_date)
    return ws_case_id, ws_open_date, ws_case_status, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

#       17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID.
#           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
#           COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
#           STRING 'CS' DELIMITED SIZE
#                  WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
#                  WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
#              INTO WS-CASE-ID.
def generate_case_id() -> str:
    """Generate a unique case ID."""
    ws_date_part = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    ws_random_part = int(random() * 99999)
    ws_case_id = f'CS{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}'
    return ws_case_id

from random import random

#       17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.
#           EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
#              WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
#                 MOVE 2 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
#              WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
#                 MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
#              WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
#                 MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
#              WHEN 'GENERAL INQUIRY'
#                 MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
#              WHEN OTHER
#                 MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
#           END-EVALUATE
#           COMPUTE WS-TARGET-DATE = 
#              FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-OPEN-DATE) +
#              WS-CASE-PRIORITY * 2.
def categorize_case(ws_case_type: str, ws_open_date: date) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Categorize the case based on its type and calculate the target date."""
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 2
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 3
        case _:
            ws_case_priority = 3
    
    ws_target_date = ws_open_date.toordinal() + ws_case_priority * 2
    return ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

#       17200-ROUTE-CASE.
#           EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
#              WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
#                 MOVE 'BILLING' TO WS-QUEUE
#              WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
#                 MOVE 'FRAUD' TO WS-QUEUE
#              WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
#                 MOVE 'SECURITY' TO WS-QUEUE
#              WHEN 'LOAN INQUIRY'
#                 MOVE 'LENDING' TO WS-QUEUE
#              WHEN OTHER
#                 MOVE 'GENERAL' TO WS-QUEUE
#           END-EVALUATE
#           PERFORM 17210-ASSIGN-AGENT.
def route_case(ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Route the case to the appropriate queue."""
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_queue = 'BILLING'
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_queue = 'FRAUD'
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_queue = 'SECURITY'
        case 'LOAN INQUIRY':
            ws_queue = 'LENDING'
        case _:
            ws_queue = 'GENERAL'
    
    ws_assigned_agent = assign_agent(ws_queue)
    return ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent

#       17210-ASSIGN-AGENT.
#           CALL 'ROUTECASE' USING WS-QUEUE WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT
#           IF WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT = SPACES
#              MOVE 'UNASSIGNED

def integer_of_date(date: str) -> int:
    """Dummy function for INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
    return 0

    """Dummy function for MOD"""
    return a % b

    """Dummy function for RANDOM"""
    return random.random()
