    """Dummy PIN encryption"""
    return "ENCRYPTED_" + pin

def pinverify(card_number: str, pin: str) -> str:
    """Dummy PIN verification"""
    if pin == "1234":
        return "MATCH"
    else:
        return "NOMATCH"

def string(*args, delimiter: str = "") -> str:
  """Simulates COBOL STRING statement."""
  result = ""
  for arg in args:
    result += str(arg)
  return result

class DataAnalytics:

    def __init__(self, ws_record: WSRecord = WSRecord(), trans_rec: TransactionRecord = TransactionRecord(), cust_rec: CustomerRecord = CustomerRecord(), perf_rec: PerformanceRecord = PerformanceRecord(), daily_rec: DailySummaryRecord = DailySummaryRecord(), weekly_rec: WeeklySummaryRecord = WeeklySummaryRecord(), monthly_rec: MonthlySummaryRecord = MonthlySummaryRecord(), dashboard_rec: DashboardRecord = DashboardRecord(), account_rec: AccountRecord = AccountRecord(), escheat_rec: EscheatRecord = EscheatRecord(), check_rec: CheckRecord = CheckRecord(), archive_rec: ArchiveRecord = ArchiveRecord(), card_rec: CardRecord = CardRecord(), shipment_rec: ShipmentRecord = ShipmentRecord()):
        self.ws_record = ws_record
        self.trans_rec = trans_rec
        self.cust_rec = cust_rec
        self.perf_rec = perf_rec
        self.daily_rec = daily_rec
        self.weekly_rec = weekly_rec
        self.monthly_rec = monthly_rec
        self.dashboard_rec = dashboard_rec
        self.account_rec = account_rec
        self.escheat_rec = escheat_rec
        self.check_rec = check_rec
        self.archive_rec = archive_rec
        self.card_rec = card_rec
        self.shipment_rec = shipment_rec

    def determine_next_run_date(self, ws_last_run_date: str, run_frequency: str) -> str:
        """
        Determine the next run date based on the run frequency.

        Args:
            ws_last_run_date: The last run date.
            run_frequency: The run frequency ('DAILY', 'WEEKLY', 'MONTHLY', 'QUARTERLY', 'YEARLY').

        Returns:
            The next run date.
        """
        match run_frequency:
            case 'DAILY':
                # 00001 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
                next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 1
            case 'WEEKLY':
                # 00003 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
                next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 7
            case 'MONTHLY':
                # 00005 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
                next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 30
            case 'QUARTERLY':
                # 00007 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
                next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 90
            case 'YEARLY':
                # 00009 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
                next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws_last_run_date) + 365
            case _:
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid run frequency: {run_frequency}")
        return str(next_run_date)

    def data_analytics(self):
        """21000-DATA-ANALYTICS.
           Data analytics and reporting procedures."""
        self.collect_metrics() # 21001 PERFORM 21100-COLLECT-METRICS
        self.aggregate_data() # 21002 PERFORM 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA
        self.calculate_kpi() # 21003 PERFORM 21300-CALCULATE-KPI
        self.generate_dashboard() # 21004 PERFORM 21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD
        self.export_data() # 21005 PERFORM 21500-EXPORT-DATA

    def collect_metrics(self):
        """21100-COLLECT-METRICS.
           Collects various metrics."""
        self.collect_transaction_metrics() # 21101 PERFORM 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
        self.collect_customer_metrics() # 21102 PERFORM 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
        self.collect_performance_metrics() # 21103 PERFORM 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS

    def collect_transaction_metrics(self):
        """21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
           Collects transaction-related metrics."""
        self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # 21111 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
        self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_count = 0 # 21112 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
        self.ws_record.ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # 21113 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
        while self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag != 'Y': # 21114 PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            trans_rec = read_transaction_file() # 21115 READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
            if trans_rec is None: # 21116 AT END
                self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'Y' # 21117 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else: # 21118 NOT AT END
                self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_count += 1 # 21119 ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
                self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_amount += trans_rec.trans_amount # 21120 ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
        if self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_count > 0: # 21124 IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
            # 21125 COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT =  WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
            self.ws_record.ws_avg_trans_amount = self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_amount / self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_count
        self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # 21128 MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def collect_customer_metrics(self):
        """21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS.
           Collects customer-related metrics."""
        self.ws_record.ws_active_customers = 0 # 21129 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
        self.ws_record.ws_new_customers = 0 # 21130 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
        self.ws_record.ws_churned_customers = 0 # 21131 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
        while self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag != 'Y': # 21132 PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            cust_rec = read_customer_file() # 21133 READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
            if cust_rec is None: # 21134 AT END
                self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'Y' # 21135 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else: # 21136 NOT AT END
                if cust_rec.cust_status == 'A': # 21137 IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
                    self.ws_record.ws_active_customers += 1 # 21138 ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
                if cust_rec.cust_open_date >= self.ws_record.ws_period_start: # 21140 IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                    self.ws_record.ws_new_customers += 1 # 21141 ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
                if cust_rec.cust_close_date >= self.ws_record.ws_period_start: # 21143 IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                    self.ws_record.ws_churned_customers += 1 # 21144 ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
        self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # 21147 MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def collect_performance_metrics(self):
        """21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.
           Collects performance-related metrics."""
        self.ws_record.ws_response_time_total = Decimal("0.00") # 21148 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
        self.ws_record.ws_response_count = 0 # 21149 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        while self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag != 'Y': # 21150 PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            perf_rec = read_perf_log_file() # 21151 READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
            if perf_rec is None: # 21152 AT END
                self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'Y' # 21153 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else: # 21154 NOT AT END
                self.ws_record.ws_response_time_total += perf_rec.perf_response_time # 21155 ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
                self.ws_record.ws_response_count += 1 # 21156 ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        if self.ws_record.ws_response_count > 0: # 21159 IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
            # 21160 COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME =  WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
            self.ws_record.ws_avg_response_time = self.ws_record.ws_response_time_total / self.ws_record.ws_response_count
        self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # 21162 MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def aggregate_data(self):
        """21200-AGGREGATE-DATA.
           Aggregates data."""
        self.daily_aggregation() # 21201 PERFORM 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
        self.weekly_aggregation() # 21202 PERFORM 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
        self.monthly_aggregation() # 21203 PERFORM 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION

    def daily_aggregation(self):
        """21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION.
           Aggregates data on a daily basis."""
        daily_summary = DailySummaryRecord() # 21211 INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
        daily_summary.daily_date = self.ws_record.ws_process_date # 21212 MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
        daily_summary.daily_trans_count = self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_count # 21213 MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
        daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = self.ws_record.ws_total_trans_amount # 21214 MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        daily_summary.daily_deposits = self.ws_record.ws_total_deposits # 21215 MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
        daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = self.ws_record.ws_total_withdrawals # 21216 MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS
        write_daily_summary_record(daily_summary) # 21217 WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY

    def weekly_aggregation(self):
        """21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION.
           Aggregates data on a weekly basis."""
        if self.ws_record.ws_day_of_week == 7: # 21218 IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
            weekly_summary = WeeklySummaryRecord() # 21219 INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
            weekly_summary.weekly_week = self.ws_record.ws_week_number # 21220 MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
            self.sum_week_data(weekly_summary) # 21221 PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
            write_weekly_summary_record(weekly_summary) # 21222 WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY

    def sum_week_data(self, weekly_summary: WeeklySummaryRecord):
        """21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA.
           Sums the data for the week."""
        weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0 # 21226 MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
        weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # 21227 MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        for _ in range(7): # 21228 PERFORM 7 TIMES
            daily_summary = DailySummaryRecord() # Assuming a DailySummaryRecord object exists
            weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += daily_summary.daily_trans_count # 21229 ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
            weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += daily_summary.daily_trans_amount # 21230 ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT

    def monthly_aggregation(self):
        """21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.
           Aggregates data on a monthly basis."""
        if self.ws_record.ws_end_of_month == 'Y': # 21231 IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
            monthly_summary = MonthlySummaryRecord() # 21232 INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
            monthly_summary.monthly_month = self.ws_record.ws_curr_month # 21233 MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
            monthly_summary.monthly_year = self.ws_record.ws_curr_year # 21234 MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
            self.sum_month_data(monthly_summary) # 21235 PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
            write_monthly_summary_record(monthly_summary) # 21236 WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY

    def sum_month_data(self, monthly_summary: MonthlySummaryRecord):
        """21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA.
           Sums the data for the month."""
        monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0 # 21237 MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
        monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # 21238 MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0 # 21239 MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
        monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0 # 21240 MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS
        self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        while self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag != 'Y': # 21241 PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file() # 21242 READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
            if daily_sum_rec is None: # 21243 AT END
                self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'Y' # 21244 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else: # 21245 NOT AT END
                if daily_sum_rec.daily_month == self.ws_record.ws_curr_month: # 21246 IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
                    monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count # 21247 ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                    monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount # 21248 ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        self.ws_record.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # 21251 MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def calculate_kpi(self):
        """21300-CALCULATE-KPI.
           Calculates Key Performance Indicators (KPIs)."""
        self.calc_financial_kpi() # 21301 PERFORM 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
        self.calc_operational_kpi() # 21302 PERFORM 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
        self.calc_customer_kpi() # 21303 PERFORM 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI

    def calc_financial_kpi(self):
        """21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI.
           Calculates financial KPIs."""
        if self.ws_record.ws_total_assets > 0: # 21311 IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
            # 21312 COMPUTE WS-ROA =  (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
            self.ws_record.ws_roa = (self.ws_record.ws_net_income / self.ws_record.ws_total_assets) * 100
        if self.ws_record.ws_total_equity > 0: # 21314 IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
            # 21315 COMPUTE WS-ROE =  (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
            self.ws_record.ws_roe = (self.ws_record.ws_net_income / self.ws_record.ws_total_equity) * 100

# === CHUNK 11 (lines 6681-7348) ===

    def process_shipping(self):
        """Implements the shipping logic."""
        if self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE > 1000:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS'  # MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO SHIP-METHOD
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2  # COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 2
        else:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD'  # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO SHIP-METHOD
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 7  # COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 7
        self.write_shipment_record(self.WS_SHIPMENT_RECORD) # WRITE SHIPMENT-RECORD FROM WS-SHIPMENT-RECORD.

    def integer_of_date(self, ws_date: str) -> int:
        """Converts YYYYMMDD date to integer format."""
        return int(ws_date)

    def card_blocking(self):
        """23500-CARD-BLOCKING."""
        self.CARD_STATUS = 'B' # MOVE 'B' TO CARD-STATUS
        self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON = self.WS_BLOCK_REASON # MOVE WS-BLOCK-REASON TO CARD-BLOCK-REASON
        self.CARD_BLOCK_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO CARD-BLOCK-DATE
        self.rewrite_card_record(self.WS_CARD_RECORD) # REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'CARD-BLOCKED' # MOVE 'CARD-BLOCKED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS' # MOVE 'SMS' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {self.WS_BLOCK_REASON}' # STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-BLOCK-REASON DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-BODY
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.

    def send_notification(self):
        """15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION."""
        pass  # Placeholder for actual send notification logic

    def wire_transfer(self):
        """24000-WIRE-TRANSFER."""
        self.validate_wire_request() # PERFORM 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST
        if self.WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
            self.ofac_screening() # PERFORM 24200-OFAC-SCREENING
            if self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
                self.process_wire() # PERFORM 24300-PROCESS-WIRE
                self.send_confirmation() # PERFORM 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION
            else:
                self.reject_wire() # PERFORM 24500-REJECT-WIRE

    def validate_wire_request(self):
        """24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT' # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == ' ':
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' # MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
            self.WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED

    def ofac_screening(self):
        """24200-OFAC-SCREENING."""
        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
        self.ofac_search(self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE) # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH' # MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
        self.ofac_search(self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE) # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH' # MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT

    def ofac_search(self, ofac_request: OfacRequest, ofac_response: OfacResponse):
        """Placeholder for OFACSRCH call."""
        pass

    def process_wire(self):
        """24300-PROCESS-WIRE."""
        self.debit_originator() # PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
        self.create_wire_message() # PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
        self.transmit_wire() # PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
        self.record_wire() # PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE

    def debit_originator(self):
        """24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_FEE # SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.update_account() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT

    def update_account(self):
        """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        pass

    def create_wire_message(self):
        """24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE."""
        ws_swift_message = WsSwiftMessage() # INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103' # MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_TXN_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_VALUE_DATE = self.WS_WIRE_DATE # MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_CURRENCY = self.WS_WIRE_CURRENCY # MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_BENEF_CUST = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
        ws_swift_message.SWIFT_REMIT_INFO = self.WS_PURPOSE # MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO
        self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = ws_swift_message

    def transmit_wire(self):
        """24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE."""
        self.swift_send(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, self.WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE) # CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
        if self.SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT' # MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        else:
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED' # MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
            self.reverse_debit() # PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT

    def swift_send(self, swift_message: WsSwiftMessage, swift_response: WsSwiftResponse):
        """Placeholder for SWIFTSEND call."""
        pass

    def reverse_debit(self):
        """24350-REVERSE-DEBIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_FEE # ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.update_account() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT

    def record_wire(self):
        """24340-RECORD-WIRE."""
        ws_wire_record = WsWireRecord() # INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
        ws_wire_record.WIRE_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
        ws_wire_record.WIRE_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
        ws_wire_record.WIRE_STATUS = self.WS_WIRE_STATUS # MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
        ws_wire_record.WIRE_FROM_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
        ws_wire_record.WIRE_TO_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
        ws_wire_record.WIRE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD = ws_wire_record
        self.write_wire_record(self.WS_WIRE_RECORD) # WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD

    def write_wire_record(self, record: WsWireRecord):
        """Writes a wire record."""
        pass  # Placeholder for actual writing logic

    def send_confirmation(self):
        """24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM' # MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' # MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {self.WS_WIRE_REF} completed' # STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def reject_wire(self):
        """24500-REJECT-WIRE."""
        self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED' # MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        ws_wire_reject_rec = WsWireRejectRec() # INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        ws_wire_reject_rec.REJECT_WIRE_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
        ws_wire_reject_rec.REJECT_REASON = self.WS_WIRE_REJECT # MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
        ws_wire_reject_rec.REJECT_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC = ws_wire_reject_rec
        self.write_wire_reject_record(self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC) # WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-REJECTED' # MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def write_wire_reject_record(self, record: WsWireRejectRec):
        """Writes a wire reject record."""
        pass  # Placeholder for actual writing logic

    def ach_processing(self):
        """25000-ACH-PROCESSING."""
        self.receive_ach_file() # PERFORM 25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE
        self.validate_ach_entries() # PERFORM 25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES
        self.process_ach_credits() # PERFORM 25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS
        self.process_ach_debits() # PERFORM 25400-PROCESS-ACH-DEBITS
        self.generate_ach_return() # PERFORM 25500-GENERATE-ACH-RETURN

    def receive_ach_file(self):
        """25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE."""
        self.open_input_ach_file() # OPEN INPUT ACH-INPUT-FILE
        self.read_ach_input_file_header()# READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-FILE-HEADER
        self.WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE = self.ACH_FILE_ID # MOVE ACH-FILE-ID TO WS-CURRENT-ACH-FILE
        self.WS_ACH_FILE_DATE = self.ACH_CREATION_DATE # MOVE ACH-CREATION-DATE TO WS-ACH-FILE-DATE
        self.WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES = self.ACH_ENTRY_COUNT # MOVE ACH-ENTRY-COUNT TO WS-EXPECTED-ENTRIES

    def open_input_ach_file(self):
        """Opens the ACH input file."""
        pass # Placeholder

    def read_ach_input_file_header(self):
        """Reads the ACH input file header."""
        pass # Placeholder

    def validate_ach_entries(self):
        """25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES."""
        self.WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
        self.WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            ach_entry = self.read_ach_input_file_entry() #READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-ENTRY
            if ach_entry is None: # AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else: # NOT AT END
                self.WS_ACH_ENTRY = ach_entry
                self.validate_single_entry() # PERFORM 25210-VALIDATE-SINGLE-ENTRY
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def read_ach_input_file_entry(self) -> Optional[WsAchEntry]:
        """Reads ACH entry from ACH input file."""
        pass

    def validate_single_entry(self):
        """25210-VALIDATE-SINGLE-ENTRY."""
        self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
        if not self.ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric(): # IF ACH-ROUTING NOT NUMERIC
            self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03' # MOVE 'R03' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
        if self.ACH_ACCOUNT == ' ': # IF ACH-ACCOUNT = SPACES
            self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04' # MOVE 'R04' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
        if self.ACH_AMOUNT <= 0: # IF ACH-AMOUNT <= 0
            self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06' # MOVE 'R06' TO WS-ACH-RETURN-CODE
        if self.WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y': # IF WS-ACH-ENTRY-VALID = 'Y'
            self.WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
        else: # ELSE
            self.WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES

    def process_ach_credits(self):
        """25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS."""
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            ach_entry = self.read_ach_input_file_entry() #READ ACH-INPUT-FILE INTO WS-ACH-ENTRY
            if ach_entry is None: # AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else: # NOT AT END
                self.WS_ACH_ENTRY = ach_entry
                if self.ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('22', '23', '32', '33'): # IF ACH-TRANS-CODE = '22' OR '23' OR '32' OR '33'
                    self.apply_credit() # PERFORM 25310-APPLY-CREDIT
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def apply_credit(self):
        """25310-APPLY-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.ACH_ACCOUNT # MOVE ACH-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account() # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y': # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.ACH_AMOUNT # ADD ACH-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            self.update_account() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
            self.WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-CREDITS-POSTED
            self.WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += self.ACH_AMOUNT # ADD ACH-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-CREDITS
        else: # ELSE
            self.WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04' # MOVE 'R04

    def validate_stop_request(self) -> None:
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'  # 29101
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:  # 29102
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'  # 29103
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'  # 29104
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':  # 29106
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'  # 29107
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'  # 29108

    def create_stop_order(self) -> None:
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()  # 29201
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID  # 29202
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER  # 29203
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT  # 29204
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME  # 29205
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE  # 29206
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180  # 29207-29208
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'  # 29209
        self.STOP_RECORD = self.WS_STOP_RECORD  # 29210
        self.write_stop_record()

    def apply_stop_fee(self) -> None:
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE  # 29301
        self.update_account()  # 29302 PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'  # 29303
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'  # 29304
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'  # 29305-29306
        self.send_notification()  # 29307 PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.

    def safe_deposit_box(self) -> None:
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental()  # 30001 PERFORM 30100-BOX-RENTAL
        self.box_access()  # 30002 PERFORM 30200-BOX-ACCESS
        self.box_drilling()  # 30003 PERFORM 30300-BOX-DRILLING
        self.box_billing()  # 30004 PERFORM 30400-BOX-BILLING.

    def box_rental(self) -> None:
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':  # 30101
            self.check_availability()  # 30102 PERFORM 30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':  # 30103
                self.assign_box()  # 30104 PERFORM 30120-ASSIGN-BOX
                self.create_rental_agreement()  # 30105 PERFORM 30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT

    def check_availability(self) -> None:
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'  # 30111
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1  # initialization for PERFORM VARYING
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:  # 30112-30118
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':  # 30113
                if self.BOX_SIZE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:  # 30114
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'  # 30115
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX  # 30116
                    break  # EXIT PERFORM
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1 # increment for PERFORM VARYING

    def assign_box(self) -> None:
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'  # 30121
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID  # 30122
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE  # 30123

    def create_rental_agreement(self) -> None:
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()  # 30131
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX  # 30132
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID  # 30133
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE  # 30134
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[int(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE) - 1]  # 30135
        self.RENTAL_RECORD = self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT  # 30136
        self.write_rental_record()

    def box_access(self) -> None:
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':  # 30201
            self.verify_renter()  # 30202 PERFORM 30210-VERIFY-RENTER
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':  # 30203
                self.log_access()  # 30204 PERFORM 30220-LOG-ACCESS
                self.escort_to_vault()  # 30205 PERFORM 30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT

    def verify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'  # 30211
        if self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:  # 30212
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':  # 30213
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':  # 30214
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'  # 30215

    def log_access(self) -> None:
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()  # 30221
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = self.WS_BOX_NUMBER  # 30222
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID  # 30223
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE  # 30224
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = self.current_time()  # 30225
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY'  # 30226
        self.ACCESS_LOG_RECORD = self.WS_ACCESS_LOG  # 30227
        self.write_access_log_record()

    def escort_to_vault(self) -> None:
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'  # 30231
        self.display_ws_display_msg()  # 30232

    def box_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':  # 30301
            self.validate_drilling_auth()  # 30302 PERFORM 30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':  # 30303
                self.schedule_drilling()  # 30304 PERFORM 30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING
                self.notify_renter()  # 30305 PERFORM 30330-NOTIFY-RENTER

    def validate_drilling_auth(self) -> None:
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'  # 30311
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:  # 30312
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'  # 30313
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':  # 30314
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'  # 30315
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':  # 30316
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':  # 30317
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'  # 30318

    def schedule_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()  # 30321
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = self.WS_BOX_NUMBER  # 30322
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON  # 30323
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30  # 30324
        self.DRILLING_RECORD = self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD  # 30325
        self.write_drilling_record()

    def notify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'  # 30331
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'  # 30332
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'  # 30333-30334
        self.send_notification()  # 30335 PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def box_billing(self) -> None:
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1  # initialization for PERFORM VARYING
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:  # 30401-30407
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':  # 30402
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':  # 30403
                    self.charge_annual_fee()  # 30404 PERFORM 30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1 # increment for PERFORM VARYING

    def charge_annual_fee(self) -> None:
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]  # 30411
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]  # 30412
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT  # 30413
        self.update_account()  # 30414 PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000  # 30415

    def merchant_services(self) -> None:
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization()  # 31001 PERFORM 31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION
        self.capture_transaction()  # 31002 PERFORM 31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION
        self.process_settlement()  # 31003 PERFORM 31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT
        self.handle_chargeback()  # 31004 PERFORM 31400-HANDLE-CHARGEBACK

    def process_authorization(self) -> None:
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card()  # 31101 PERFORM 31110-VALIDATE-CARD
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':  # 31102
            self.check_fraud_score()  # 31103 PERFORM 31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':  # 31104
                self.check_available_credit()  # 31105 PERFORM 31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':  # 31106
                    self.approve_auth()  # 31107 PERFORM 31140-APPROVE-AUTH
                else:  # 31108
                    self.decline_auth()  # 31109 PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH
            else:  # 31110
                self.decline_auth()  # 31111 PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH
        else:  # 31112
            self.decline_auth()  # 31113 PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH

    def validate_card(self) -> None:
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'  # 31111
        self.check_luhn()  # 31112 PERFORM 31115-CHECK-LUHN
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':  # 31113
            self.check_expiry()  # 31114 PERFORM 31116-CHECK-EXPIRY
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':  # 31115
                self.check_cvv()  # 31116 PERFORM 31117-CHECK-CVV
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':  # 31117
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'  # 31118

    def check_luhn(self) -> None:
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0  # 31116
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16  # initialization for PERFORM VARYING
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:  # 31117-31126
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1]) # 31118
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:  # 31119
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2  # 31120
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:  # 31121
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9  # 31122
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT  # 31124
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1 # decrement for PERFORM VARYING
        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:  # 31127
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'  #

        self.RECON_REPORT_RECORD: ReconReportRecord = ReconReportRecord()
        self.WS_SUB_DETAIL: WsSubDetail = WsSubDetail()
        self.SUBLEDGER_FILE: SubledgerFile = SubledgerFile()

    def update_cfp_document(self):
        """33330-UPDATE-CFP-DOCUMENT."""
        self.WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE = date.today() # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CFP-UPDATE-DATE
        self.CFP_RECORD.cfp_overall_status = self.WS_CFP_STATUS # MOVE WS-CFP-STATUS TO CFP-OVERALL-STATUS
        self.CFP_RECORD.cfp_total_sources = self.WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING # MOVE WS-AVAILABLE-FUNDING TO CFP-TOTAL-SOURCES
        self.CFP_RECORD.cfp_stress_needs = self.WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS # MOVE WS-STRESSED-OUTFLOWS TO CFP-STRESS-NEEDS
        self.rewrite_cfp_record(self.WS_CFP_DOCUMENT) # REWRITE CFP-RECORD FROM WS-CFP-DOCUMENT

    def capital_management(self):
        """34000-CAPITAL-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.calculate_capital_ratios() # PERFORM 34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS
        self.risk_weighted_assets() # PERFORM 34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.capital_planning() # PERFORM 34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING
        self.stress_testing() # PERFORM 34400-STRESS-TESTING

    def calculate_capital_ratios(self):
        """34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS."""
        self.calculate_tier1() # PERFORM 34110-CALCULATE-TIER1
        self.calculate_tier2() # PERFORM 34120-CALCULATE-TIER2
        self.calculate_ratios() # PERFORM 34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS

    def calculate_tier1(self):
        """34110-CALCULATE-TIER1."""
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_COMMON_STOCK # ADD WS-COMMON-STOCK TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS # ADD WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_AOCI # ADD WS-AOCI TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_GOODWILL # SUBTRACT WS-GOODWILL FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_INTANGIBLES # SUBTRACT WS-INTANGIBLES FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_DTA_DEDUCTION # SUBTRACT WS-DTA-DEDUCTION FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL

    def calculate_tier2(self):
        """34120-CALCULATE-TIER2."""
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_SUB_DEBT # ADD WS-SUB-DEBT TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE # ADD WS-ALLL-ELIGIBLE TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
        self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL # COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL = WS-TIER1-CAPITAL + WS-TIER2-CAPITAL

    def calculate_ratios(self):
        """34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS."""
        if self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS > 0: # IF WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS > 0
            self.WS_CET1_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-CET1-RATIO = (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
            self.WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO = (WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
        if self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0: # IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
            self.WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-LEVERAGE-RATIO = (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100

    def risk_weighted_assets(self):
        """34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS."""
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.credit_rwa() # PERFORM 34210-CREDIT-RWA
        self.market_rwa() # PERFORM 34220-MARKET-RWA
        self.operational_rwa() # PERFORM 34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA

    def credit_rwa(self):
        """34210-CREDIT-RWA."""
        self.WS_CASH_RWA = self.WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00") # COMPUTE WS-CASH-RWA = WS-CASH-POSITION * 0.00
        self.WS_GOVT_RWA = self.WS_GOVT_SECURITIES * Decimal("0.00") # COMPUTE WS-GOVT-RWA = WS-GOVT-SECURITIES * 0.00
        self.WS_BANK_RWA = self.WS_BANK_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.20") # COMPUTE WS-BANK-RWA = WS-BANK-DEPOSITS * 0.20
        self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA = self.WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES * Decimal("0.50") # COMPUTE WS-MORTGAGE-RWA = WS-RESIDENTIAL-MORTGAGES * 0.50
        self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA = self.WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS * Decimal("1.00") # COMPUTE WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA = WS-COMMERCIAL-LOANS * 1.00
        self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA = self.WS_CONSUMER_LOANS * Decimal("1.00") # COMPUTE WS-CONSUMER-RWA = WS-CONSUMER-LOANS * 1.00
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CASH_RWA # ADD WS-CASH-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_GOVT_RWA # ADD WS-GOVT-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_BANK_RWA # ADD WS-BANK-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA # ADD WS-MORTGAGE-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA # ADD WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA # ADD WS-CONSUMER-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS

    def market_rwa(self):
        """34220-MARKET-RWA."""
        self.WS_MARKET_RWA = self.WS_TRADING_ASSETS * self.WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR # COMPUTE WS-MARKET-RWA = WS-TRADING-ASSETS * WS-MARKET-RISK-FACTOR
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MARKET_RWA # ADD WS-MARKET-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS

    def operational_rwa(self):
        """34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA."""
        self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA = self.WS_GROSS_INCOME * self.WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR * Decimal("12.5") # COMPUTE WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA = WS-GROSS-INCOME * WS-OPERATIONAL-FACTOR * 12.5
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA # ADD WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS

    def capital_planning(self):
        """34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING."""
        self.project_capital_needs() # PERFORM 34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS
        self.

def main():
    """Main function to execute the program"""
    global WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL, WS_RECON_DIFF
    WS_RECON_DIFF = WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal('0.00'):
        log_recon_exception()

    intercompany_recon()
    nostro_recon()
    audit_trail()
    performance_monitoring()
    disaster_recovery()
    security_procedures()
    crm_procedures()
    end_program()

def log_recon_exception():
    """37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION.
       INITIALIZE WS-RECON-EXCEPTION
       MOVE WS-GL-ACCOUNT TO RECON-EXC-ACCOUNT
       MOVE WS-RECON-DIFF TO RECON-EXC-DIFF
       MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO RECON-EXC-DATE
       WRITE RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD FROM WS-RECON-EXCEPTION."""
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_OBJ, WS_GL_ACCOUNT, WS_RECON_DIFF, RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_OBJ = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION()
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_OBJ.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT = WS_GL_ACCOUNT
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_OBJ.RECON_EXC_DIFF = WS_RECON_DIFF
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_OBJ.RECON_EXC_DATE = str(datetime.now())
    RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD.append(WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_OBJ)

def intercompany_recon():
    """37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON.
       PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES
       PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS
       PERFORM 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES."""
    load_ic_balances()
    match_ic_pairs()
    report_ic_differences()

def load_ic_balances():
    """37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES.
       MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
          READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
             AT END
                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
             NOT AT END
                ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
                MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO 
                   WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
          END-READ
       END-PERFORM
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, INTERCOMPANY_FILE, WS_IC_ARRAY
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_IC_BALANCE_READ = INTERCOMPANY_FILE[WS_IC_COUNT]
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = WS_IC_BALANCE_READ # Assuming INTERCOMPANY_FILE contains objects
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs():
    """37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS.
       PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
          UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
          PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
       END-PERFORM."""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        find_ic_counterpart()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def find_ic_counterpart():
    """37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART.
       MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
       MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO
       PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1
          UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
          IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
             IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
                COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF = 
                   IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) + 
                   IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                   PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                END-IF
                EXIT PERFORM
             END-IF
          END-IF
       END-PERFORM."""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY
    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_FROM_ENTITY
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_TO_ENTITY
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2-1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2-1].IC_TO_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX-1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2-1].IC_AMOUNT
                if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal('0.00'):
                    log_ic_diff()
                break
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

def log_ic_diff():
    """37326-LOG-IC-DIFF.
       INITIALIZE WS-IC-DIFF-REC
       MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
       MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
       MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
       WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC."""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC_OBJ, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, IC_DIFF_RECORD
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_OBJ = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_OBJ.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_OBJ.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_OBJ.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF
    IC_DIFF_RECORD.append(WS_IC_DIFF_REC_OBJ)

    """37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES.
       DISPLAY 'INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'."""
    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

def nostro_recon():
    """37400-NOSTRO-RECON.
       PERFORM 37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT
       PERFORM 37420-MATCH-NOSTRO-ENTRIES
       PERFORM 37430-GENERATE-NOSTRO-REPORT."""
    load_nostro_statement()
    match_nostro_entries()
    generate_nostro_report()

def load_nostro_statement():
    """37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT.
       MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
          READ NOSTRO-STATEMENT-FILE INTO WS-NOSTRO-ITEM
             AT END
                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
             NOT AT END
                ADD 1 TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
          END-READ
       END-PERFORM
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    global WS_NOSTRO_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, NOSTRO_STATEMENT_FILE
    WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            NOSTRO_STATEMENT_FILE[WS_NOSTRO_COUNT]
            WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def match_nostro_entries():

    print('MATCHING NOSTRO ENTRIES')

def generate_nostro_report():

def audit_trail():
    """38000-AUDIT-TRAIL.
       PERFORM 38100-LOG-USER-ACTION
       PERFORM 38200-LOG-DATA-CHANGE
       PERFORM 38300-LOG-SYSTEM-EVENT
       PERFORM 38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS."""
    log_user_action()
    log_data_change()
    log_system_event()
    archive_audit_logs()

def log_user_action():
    """38100-LOG-USER-ACTION.
       INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
       COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
       MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
       MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
       MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
       MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
       WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD."""
    import random
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ, WS_USER_ID, WS_ACTION_TYPE, WS_SESSION_ID, AUDIT_RECORD
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(random.random() * 99999999999)
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_USER = WS_USER_ID
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = WS_ACTION_TYPE
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = WS_SESSION_ID
    AUDIT_RECORD.append(WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ)

def log_data_change():
    """38200-LOG-DATA-CHANGE.
       INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
       COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
       MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
       MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
       MOVE 'UPDATE' TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
       MOVE WS-TABLE-NAME TO WS-AUDIT-TABLE
       MOVE WS-RECORD-KEY TO WS-AUDIT-KEY
       MOVE WS-OLD-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-OLD-VALUE
       MOVE WS-NEW-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-NEW-VALUE
       WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD."""
    import random
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ, WS_USER_ID, WS_TABLE_NAME, WS_RECORD_KEY, WS_OLD_VALUE, WS_NEW_VALUE, AUDIT_RECORD
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(random.random() * 99999999999)
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_USER = WS_USER_ID
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = 'UPDATE'
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_TABLE = WS_TABLE_NAME
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_KEY = WS_RECORD_KEY
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_OLD_VALUE = WS_OLD_VALUE
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_NEW_VALUE = WS_NEW_VALUE
    AUDIT_RECORD.append(WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ)

def log_system_event():
    """38300-LOG-SYSTEM-EVENT.
       INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
       COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
       MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
       MOVE 'SYSTEM' TO WS-AUDIT-USER
       MOVE WS-EVENT-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
       WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD."""
    import random
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ, WS_EVENT_TYPE, AUDIT_RECORD
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(random.random() * 99999999999)
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_USER = 'SYSTEM'
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = WS_EVENT_TYPE
    AUDIT_RECORD.append(WS_AUDIT_RECORD_OBJ)

def archive_audit_logs():
    """38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS.
       IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
          PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
          PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE
       END-IF."""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        move_to_archive()
        compress_archive()

def move_to_archive():
    """38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE.
       PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
          READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
             AT END
                MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
             NOT AT END
                IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
                   WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD 
                      FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                   DELETE AUDIT-FILE
                END-IF
          END-READ
       END-PERFORM
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, AUDIT_FILE, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_FILE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # Ensure correct initial value
    i = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            audit_record = AUDIT_FILE[i]
            if audit_record.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                ARCHIVE_AUDIT_FILE.append(audit_record)
                del AUDIT_FILE[i]
            else:
                i+=1
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def compress_archive():
    """38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE.
       DISPLAY 'COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE'."""
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

def performance_monitoring():
    """39000-PERFORMANCE-MONITORING.
       PERFORM 39100-COLLECT-METRICS
       PERFORM 39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE
       PERFORM 39300-GENERATE-ALERTS
       PERFORM 39400-OPTIMIZE-RESOURCES."""
    collect_metrics()
    analyze_performance()
    generate_alerts()
    optimize_resources()

def collect_metrics():
    """39100-COLLECT-METRICS.
       PERFORM 39110-CPU-METRICS
       PERFORM 39120-MEMORY-METRICS
       PERFORM 39130-IO-METRICS
       PERFORM 39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
    cpu_metrics()
    memory_metrics()
    io_metrics()
    transaction_metrics()

def cpu_metrics():
    """39110-CPU-METRICS.
       CALL 'GETCPU' USING WS-CPU-UTILIZATION
       IF WS-CPU-UTILIZATION > 80
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CPU-ALERT
       END-IF."""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = getcpu()  # Assuming getcpu() is defined elsewhere
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

def memory_metrics():
    """39120-MEMORY-METRICS.
       CALL 'GETMEM' USING WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION
       IF WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION > 85
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MEMORY-ALERT
       END-IF."""
    global WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION, WS_MEMORY_ALERT
    WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION = getmem()  # Assuming getmem() is defined elsewhere
    if WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION > 85:
        WS_MEMORY_ALERT = 'Y'

def io_metrics():
    """39130-IO-METRICS.
       CALL 'GETIO' USING WS-IO-WAIT-TIME
       IF WS-IO-WAIT-TIME > WS-IO-THRESHOLD
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-IO-ALERT
       END-IF."""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIME, WS_IO_THRESHOLD, WS_IO_ALERT
    WS_IO_WAIT_TIME = getio()  # Assuming getio() is defined elsewhere
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIME > WS_IO_THRESHOLD:
        WS_IO_ALERT = 'Y'

def transaction_metrics():
    """39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
       COMPUTE WS-TPS = 
          WS-TRANS-COUNT / WS-ELAPSED-SECONDS
       COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE = 
          WS-TOTAL-RESPONSE-TIME / WS-TRANS-COUNT."""
    global WS_TPS, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME
    WS_TPS = Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) / Decimal(WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS) if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS != 0 else Decimal('0.00')
    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME) / Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) if WS_TRANS_COUNT != 0 else Decimal('0.00')

def analyze_performance():
    """39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE.
       IF WS-AVG-RESPONSE > WS-RESPONSE-THRESHOLD
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PERF-DEGRADED
       END-IF
       IF WS-TPS < WS-MIN-TPS-THRESHOLD
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-THROUGHPUT-LOW
       END-IF."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW
    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'

def generate_alerts():
    """39300-GENERATE-ALERTS.
       IF WS-CPU-ALERT = 'Y'
          PERFORM 39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT
       END-IF
       IF WS-MEMORY-ALERT = 'Y'
          PERFORM 39320-SEND-MEMORY-ALERT
       END-IF
       IF WS-PERF-DEGRADED = 'Y'
          PERFORM 39330-SEND-PERF-ALERT
       END-IF."""
    global WS_CPU_ALERT, WS_MEMORY_ALERT, WS_PERF_DEGRADED
    if WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_cpu_alert()
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_memory_alert()
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y':
        send_perf_alert()

def send_cpu_alert():
    """39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT.
       MOVE 'HIGH-CPU' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
       MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
       STRING 'ALERT: CPU utilization at ' DELIMITED SIZE
              WS-CPU-UTILIZATION DELIMITED SIZE
              '%' DELIMITED SIZE
          INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
       PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CPU_UTILIZATION
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'HIGH-CPU'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'ALERT: CPU utilization at {WS_CPU_UTILIZATION}%'
    send_notification()

def send_memory_alert():
    """39320-SEND-MEMORY-ALERT.
       MOVE 'HIGH-MEMORY' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
       MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
       MOVE 'ALERT: High memory utilization' 
          TO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
       PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'HIGH-MEMORY'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'ALERT: High memory utilization'
    send_notification()

def send_perf_alert():
    """39330-SEND-PERF-ALERT.
       MOVE 'PERFORMANCE' TO
